```
def fft_convolve(a,b=None):
    if b is None: b=a
    n=2*len(a)-1
    F=1 << (n-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.rfft(a,F)
    fb=np.rfft(b,F) if b is not a else fa
    return np.irfft(fa*fb,F)[:n]
```
Small negative from FFT set max with 0? For nonneg convolution, negative tiny; use `np.maximum(out,0)` maybe changes gradient slightly but fine. For score max, use out.max(); if tiny negative no issue. For gradient weights, c should be nonnegative; clip to 0.

Algorithm details for block stage:
```
def build_u_block(z, M, L, n):
    h = softmax(z)
    u = np.empty(n)
    for i: u[i*L:(i+1)*L] = h[i] / L
    return u, h
```

Detailed code plan:
```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft

height_sequence_1 available.

def next_pow2(n): return 1 << (n-1).bit_length()

def conv_fft(a):
    n_a = a.size
    n = 2*n_a -1
    F = next_pow2(n)
    A = rfft(a, F)
    out = irfft(A*A, F)[:n]
    # remove tiny negative
    if out.min() < 0: out = np.maximum(out,0) # maybe costly but fine
    return out

def score_fft(u, n):
    c=conv_fft(u)
    return 2*n*c.max()
```
But clipping out after every conv may hide negative numeric; gradient weights should use nonnegative. Use `out[np.abs(out)<1e-12]=0`? Could be overhead. Since u nonneg, negative small. For max, `out.max()` unaffected if negative. For weights, exp negative small negligible; ok. Don't clip to avoid overhead. But if negative -1e-18, exp ok.

Softmax stable:
```
def softmax(z):
    z = z - z.max()
    # clip z to avoid underflow? If range >50, exp small; okay. But too large differences -> zeros; that's fine.
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = e.sum()
    if s ==0 or not finite: return uniform
    return e/s
```
Need store z maybe with mean zero but large range. Re-center each iter: `z -= z.mean()` but if large range, mean shift no effect on softmax; re-center to prevent overflow. Also clip `z = np.clip(z, -50, 50)`? If clip after recenter, can flatten extremes. Maybe use dynamic: if z.max()-z.min()>60, rescale? Since h ratio >1e26 possible but not needed; clip to 60. But affine transformations that preserve center? We can `z = np.clip(z, -60, 60)`. This may slightly alter objective but fine.

Adam:
```
def adam_update(z, grad, m, v, t, lr):
    m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*grad
    v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*grad*grad
    mhat = m/(1-beta1**t); vhat=v/(1-beta2**t)
    return z + mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
```

Full soft step:
```
def full_soft_iter(z, u, lam, state, it, lr):
    c = conv_fft(u)
    r = n*c
    score = 2*n*r.max()
    # weights
    rmax = r.max()
    x = lam*(r-rmax)
    # x <=0, maybe very negative
    ex = np.exp(x)
    if ex.sum()==0: w = zeros; w[argmax]=1
    else: w=ex/ex.sum()
    L = rmax + np.log(ex.sum())/lam
    G = grad_from_w(w,u)
    dz = u*(G - np.dot(u,G))
    # Adam
    z = adam(z,dz,state,it,lr)
    z = z - z.mean(); z = np.clip(z, -80,80)
    u = softmax(z)
    # Also we could do a small line search? Since softmax step may increase score. Adam with lr may not guarantee decrease. We can compare score after step; if worse significantly, shrink? Could revert z and reduce lr.
```

Let's refine each helper.

`fft_convolve`:
```
def fft_conv(a):
    n = a.size
    F = 1 << (2*n - 2).bit_length()
    A = rfft(a, F)
    return irfft(A*A, F)[:2*n-1]
```
For n=5632, 2n-2=11262, bit_length -> 11? 2^14=16384? 11262 bit length 14 (2^13=8192, 2^14=16384). Good.

`gradient_from_w`:
```
def grad_from_weights(w, u):
    n=u.size
    F = 1 << (3*n - 3).bit_length()
    wp = np.zeros(F); wp[:len(w)] = w
    up = np.zeros(F); up[:n] = u[::-1]
    z = irfft(rfft(wp)*rfft(up), F)
    return 2.0*z[n-1:n+n-1]  # length n? z indices n-1 to n+n-2 inclusive -> n-1:n+n-1
```
Check end index: need j=0..n-1 -> positions n-1 + j, last 2n-2. Slice `n-1 : 2*n-1` length n. `z[n-1:2*n-1]` yes (because 2n-1 exclusive). Use that.
Memory: F for n=5632: 3n-3=16893, bit_length 15? 2^15=32768. Good.

`soft_loss_and_grad`:
```
def soft_r_grads(u, lam):
    c=fft_conv(u); r=n*c; rmax=r.max()
    x=lam*(r-rmax)
    # underflow fine
    ex=np.exp(x)
    s=ex.sum()
    if s == 0: w=zeros; w[argmax]=1
    else: w=ex/s
    L = rmax + np.log(s)/lam
    G = grad_from_weights(w,u)

def lp_move(u, Kmax=512, t_list=(0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2)):

    if len(idx)>Kmax: idx = top  # if too many? Actually if thr many, choose top Kmax among all, use idx= top

    return best,bests, M?

Local random perturb loop can be simple and cheap:
```
def perturb_u(u, rng):
   cand=u.copy()
   width=int(rng.integers(max(4,n//200), max(8,n//8)))
   st=rng.integers(0,n-width)
   cand[st:st+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1,size=width))
   # sometimes mirror
   if rng.random()<0.2: cand = 0.5*(cand+cand[::-1])
   cand=np.clip(cand,0,None); cand/=cand.sum(); return cand
```

Implement active index selection with stable ties:
```
def active_indices(c, Kmax, frac=0.9):
    M=c.max()
    top=np.argpartition(c, -Kmax)[-Kmax:]
    thr_idx=np.nonzero(c >= frac*M)[0]
    idx=np.unique(np.concatenate((top, thr_idx)))
    if len(idx)>Kmax:
        # keep only top Kmax by c among idx
        vals=c[idx]
        topidx=np.argsort(vals)[-Kmax:]
        idx=idx[topidx]
    return idx
```
If `c` has many above threshold (flat), top Kmax among them maybe arbitrary; fine.

Let's code `optimize_sine`:
```
def optimize_sine(n, deadline, rng):
  modes=list(range(1,6))
  best=None; bests=inf
  t = np.arange(n)/n
  sines = np.stack([np.sin(2*np.pi*m*t) for m in modes], axis=1) # shape n x K, memory 5632*5 ~1.7MB ok
  def build(a):
     f = 1.0 + sines @ a
     m = f.min()
     if m < 0:
        f = 1.0 + (f-1.0)/(1.0 - m)
     u = f / f.sum()
     u = np.clip(u,0,None)
     u /= u.sum()
     return u
  def obj(a):
     return score_fft(build(a))
  starts = [...]
  for s in starts:
    if time>deadline: break
    try:
      res = minimize(obj, s, method='Powell', options={'maxiter': 100, 'ftol':1e-5, 'xatol':1e-4})
    except: res=None
    if res.success or True:
       a=np.clip(res.x,-1.5,1.5); u=build(a); s=score_fft(u); update
    # also evaluate start
  return best,bests
```
`minimize` may call obj many times; `sines @ a` efficient. `method='Nelder-Mead'` may be better for 5D; `Powell` derivative-free. Use `method='Nelder-Mead'` initial simplex size? Could be okay. Use `method='Powell'` robust. For small dimension, either.

Block optimizer from sine start:
```
def u_to_block_z(u, M, L):
  h = np.add.reduceat(u, np.arange(0,n,L)) / L
  h = np.clip(h, 1e-12, None); h/=h.sum()
  z=np.log(h); z-=z.mean(); return np.clip(z,-80,80)
```
If h has zeros, log zero; clip to tiny.

Block optimize code with internal time:
```
def optimize_block(u_init, deadline):
  M,L,n...
  starts=[]
  if u_init is not None: starts.append(u_to_block_z(u_init,...))
  starts.append(-2.0*(t-0.5))
  starts.append(np.where(t<0.5,0.8,-0.8))
  lams=[10,25,60,120]
  iters=120
  lr=0.5? Let's use 0.2.
  for z0 in starts:
     if time>deadline: break
     z=z0.copy(); adam=Adam(M)
     for lam in lams:
       lr_scale=1.0; prevL=inf; stall=0
       for it in range(iters):
         if time>deadline: break
         u,h=build_u(z); L,G=soft_L_G(u,lam)
         if not finite L: ...
         Gh=np.add.reduceat(G, starts_idx)/L
         dz=h*(Gh - np.dot(h,Gh))
         z=adam.step(z,dz,lr*lr_scale)
         z=np.clip(z-z.mean(),-80,80)
         # simple adaptation using L? L is old u; after step not known. Skip.
       # end stage u/score update
```

Full loop:
```
def optimize_full(u_init, deadline):
  z=np.log(np.maximum(u_init,1e-300)); z-=z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-80,80)
  u=softmax(z) # maybe not exactly u_init; okay
  best=u.copy(); bests=score_fft(u)
  lams=[20,50,100,200]
  lrs=[0.1,0.05,0.03,0.02]
  for lam,lr in zip(lams,lrs):
    adam=Adam(n)
    stall=0; prev_score=bests
    while time<deadline:
       L,G = soft_L_G(u,lam)
       score=2>n? from L? We can compute rmax in soft_L_G; return rmax.
       dz = u*(G - np.dot(u,G))
       z = adam.step(z,dz,lr)
       z=np.clip(z-z.mean(),-80,80)
       u=softmax(z)
       # check score every 5 or if timer
       if it%5==0:
          s=score_fft(u)
          if s<bests: bests=s;best=u.copy(); prev_score=s; stall=0
          else:
             stall+=1
             if stall>20: break # go to next lam
             if stall%10==0: # reduce? not easy with fixed lr; could break
       it+=1
    # end stage update
  return best,bests
```

LP polish from best:
```
def polish_lp(u, deadline):
  best=u.copy(); bests=score_fft(best); Kmax=512; no_improve=0
  while time < deadline and no_improve<12:
     v,s=lp_move(best,Kmax)
     if v is not None and s < bests - 1e-9:
        best=v; bests=s; no_improve=0
     else:
        no_improve +=1
        # reduce Kmax? maybe try Kmax=256 next
     Kmax = max(128, int(Kmax*0.8))
  return best,bests
```

def random_polish(u,deadline):
 best=u;bests=score_fft(best); rng=default_rng(seed)
 for i in range(number determined by time):
   cand=best.copy()
   width=rng.integers(max(8,n//100), max(16,n//5))
   st=rng.integers(0,n-width)
   cand[st:st+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.15,width))
   if rng.random()<0.2: cand=0.5*(cand+cand[::-1])
   cand=np.clip(cand,0,None); cs=cand.sum(); if cs<0.01: continue; cand/=cs
   s=score_fft(cand)

 return best,bests

`soft_L_G(u, lam)`:
```
c = fft_conv(u)
r = len(u) * c
rmax = float(r.max())
x = lam * (r - rmax)
ex = np.exp(np.minimum(0.0, x)) # x<=0 but due numeric maybe small positive; use min to ensure no overflow
s = ex.sum()
if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
    w = np.zeros_like(r); w[int(np.argmax(r))] = 1.0
    L = rmax
else:
    w = ex / s
    L = rmax + float(np.log(s)) / lam
G = grad_from_weights(w,u)
return L, G, rmax
```

`Adam` class:
```
class Adam:
 def __init__(self, size, beta1=0.9, beta2=0.999, eps=1e-8):
   self.m=np.zeros(size); self.v=np.zeros(size); self.t=0; self.b1=...
 def step(self,z,g,lr):
   self.t+=1
   self.m=self.b1*self.m+(1-self.b1)*g
   self.v=self.b2*self.v+(1-self.b2)*g*g
   mhat=self.m/(1-self.b1**self.t)
   vhat=self.v/(1-self.b2**self.t)
   return z + lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+self.eps)
```
If g has nan, z nan. Check finite in step:
```
if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): return z
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog


class Adam:
    def __init__(self, size, beta1=0.9, beta2=0.999, eps=1e-8):
        self.m = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
        self.v = np.zeros(size, dtype=np.float64)
        self.t = 0
        self.b1 = beta1
        self.b2 = beta2
        self.eps = eps

    def step(self, z, g, lr):
        if not all(np.isfinite(x) for x in (lr, z, g)):
            return z
        g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
        if g.shape != z.shape:
            return z
        self.t += 1
        self.m = self.b1 * self.m + (1.0 - self.b1) * g
        self.v = self.b2 * self.v + (1.0 - self.b2) * (g * g)
        mhat = self.m / (1.0 - self.b1 ** self.t)
        vhat = self.v / (1.0 - self.b2 ** self.t)
        out = z + lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + self.eps)
        return np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, z)


def _next_pow2(n):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    return 1 << (n - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_conv(a):
    n = a.size
    need = 2 * n - 1
    F = _next_pow2(need)
    fa = rfft(a, F)
    out = irfft(fa * fa, F)[:need]
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    return out


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return np.ones(max(1, int(z.size)), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, int(z.size))
    z = z - z.max()
    z = np.clip(z, -80.0, 80.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones_like(z) / z.size
    return e / s


def _score_fft(u):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    if u.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(u.sum())
    if s < 1e-8 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    c = _fft_conv(u)
    cm = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        return np.inf
    return float(2 * u.size * cm / (s * s))


def _soft_r(u, lam):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    if u.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(u.sum())
    if s < 1e-8 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf
    c = _fft_conv(u)
    r = (u.size / (s * s)) * c
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(r)):
        return np.inf
    rmax = float(r.max())
    x = lam * (r - rmax)
    x = np.minimum(0.0, x)
    ex = np.exp(x)
    exsum = float(ex.sum())
    if exsum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(exsum):
        return rmax
    return rmax + float(np.log(exsum)) / float(lam)


def _grad_from_weights(w, u):
    n = u.size
    F = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    wp = np.zeros(F, dtype=np.float64)
    wp[: w.size] = w
    up = np.zeros(F, dtype=np.float64)
    up[:n] = u[::-1]
    z = irfft(rfft(wp, F) * rfft(up, F), F)
    return 2.0 * z[n - 1 : 2 * n - 1]


def _soft_loss_and_grad(u, lam):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(u.sum())
    if s < 1e-8 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf, np.zeros_like(u), np.inf
    c = _fft_conv(u)
    scale = u.size / (s * s)
    r = scale * c
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(r)):
        return np.inf, np.zeros_like(u), np.inf
    rmax = float(r.max())
    x = lam * (r - rmax)
    x = np.minimum(0.0, x)
    ex = np.exp(x)
    exsum = float(ex.sum())
    if exsum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(exsum):
        w = np.zeros_like(r)
        w[int(np.argmax(r))] = 1.0
        L = rmax
    else:
        w = ex / exsum
        L = rmax + float(np.log(exsum)) / float(lam)
    G = _grad_from_weights(w, u) * scale
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)):
        G = np.zeros_like(u)
    return float(L), G, rmax


def _build_block(z, M, L):
    h = _softmax(z)
    u = np.repeat(h, L)
    s = float(u.sum())
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        u /= s
    return u, h


def _u_to_block_z(u, M, L):
    u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
    n = u.size
    st = np.arange(0, n, L)
    h = np.add.reduceat(u, st) / float(L)
    h = np.clip(h, 1e-300, None)
    s = float(h.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        h = np.ones(M, dtype=np.float64) / float(M)
    else:
        h /= s
    z = np.log(h)
    z -= z.mean()
    return np.clip(z, -80.0, 80.0)


def _clean(u):
    try:
        u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64)
        if u.ndim != 1 or u.size == 0:
            return None
        u = np.nan_to_num(u, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        u = np.clip(u, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(u.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
            return None
        return u / s
    except Exception:
        return None


def _optimize_sine(n, deadline, rng):
    K = 5
    modes = list(range(1, K + 1))
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    S = np.stack([np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * t) for m in modes], axis=1)

    def build(a):
        a = np.clip(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), -1.25, 1.25)
        f = 1.0 + S @ a
        f = np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)
        fm = float(f.min())
        if fm < 0.0:
            den = 1.0 - fm
            if den <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(den):
                f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                f = 1.0 + (f - 1.0) / den
        f = np.clip(f, 0.0, None)
        s = float(f.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        return f / s

    def obj(a):
        return _soft_r(build(a), 40.0)

    starts = [
        [0.70, 0.00, 0.00, 0.00, 0.00],
        [0.55, 0.30, 0.00, 0.00, 0.00],
        [0.45, 0.25, -0.30, 0.20, 0.00],
        [0.35, 0.20, 0.20, 0.15, 0.10],
        [0.25, -0.25, 0.35, 0.10, -0.20],
    ]
    for _ in range(3):
        starts.append(rng.normal(0.0, 0.28, K).tolist())

    best_u = None
    best_s = np.inf
    for st in starts:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        st = np.clip(np.asarray(st, dtype=np.float64), -1.25, 1.25)
        u0 = build(st)
        s0 = _score_fft(u0)
        if s0 < best_s:
            best_s, best_u = s0, u0
        try:
            res = minimize(obj, st, method="Powell",
                           options={"maxiter": 80, "ftol": 1e-6, "xatol": 1e-4})
            if res is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                continue
            a = np.clip(res.x, -1.25, 1.25)
            u1 = build(a)
            s1 = _score_fft(u1)
            if s1 < best_s:
                best_s, best_u = s1, u1
        except Exception:
            pass
    return best_u, best_s


def _optimize_block(u_init, M, L, deadline):
    n = M * L
    best_u = None
    best_s = np.inf

    if u_init is not None and u_init.size == n:
        best_u = np.asarray(u_init, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        s = float(best_u.sum())
        if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
            best_u /= s
            best_s = _score_fft(best_u)

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, M, dtype=np.float64)
    z_starts = []
    if u_init is not None and u_init.size == n:
        z_starts.append(_u_to_block_z(u_init, M, L))
    z_starts.append(np.zeros(M, dtype=np.float64))
    z_starts.append(-2.2 * (t - 0.5))
    z_starts.append(-3.0 * (t - 0.5) + 0.6 * ((t - 0.5) ** 3))
    z_starts.append(np.where(t < 0.5, 0.7, -0.7))

    st_idx = np.arange(0, n, L)
    lams = [8.0, 20.0, 55.0, 120.0]
    iters_per_lam = 120
    lr = 0.22

    for z0 in z_starts:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        z = np.asarray(z0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        z = np.clip(z - z.mean(), -80.0, 80.0)
        for lam in lams:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            adam = Adam(M)
            for _ in range(iters_per_lam):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    break
                u, h = _build_block(z, M, L)
                if float(h.max()) <= 0.0:
                    break
                Lsoft, G, rmax = _soft_loss_and_grad(u, lam)
                if not np.isfinite(Lsoft):
                    break
                if 2.0 * rmax < best_s:
                    best_s = 2.0 * rmax
                    best_u = u.copy()
                Gh = np.add.reduceat(G, st_idx) / float(L)
                dz = h * (Gh - float(np.dot(h, Gh)))
                z = adam.step(z, dz, lr)
                z = np.clip(z - z.mean(), -80.0, 80.0)
            u, _ = _build_block(z, M, L)
            s = _score_fft(u)
            if s < best_s:
                best_s, best_u = s, u
    return best_u, best_s


def _optimize_full(u_init, deadline):
    if u_init is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = u_init.size
    u0 = np.asarray(u_init, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    s = float(u0.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None, np.inf
    u0 /= s

    z = np.log(np.clip(u0, 1e-300, None))
    z = np.clip(z - z.mean(), -80.0, 80.0)
    u = _softmax(z)
    u /= u.sum()

    best_u = u0.copy()
    best_s = _score_fft(u0)

    stage_lams = [
        (18.0, 0.055),
        (45.0, 0.030),
        (90.0, 0.018),
        (200.0, 0.012),
    ]

    for lam, lr in stage_lams:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        adam = Adam(n)
        stall = 0
        last_best = best_s
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            Lsoft, G, rmax = _soft_loss_and_grad(u, lam)
            if not np.isfinite(Lsoft):
                break
            if 2.0 * rmax < best_s:
                best_s = 2.0 * rmax
                best_u = u.copy()
                last_best = best_s
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 1
                if stall > 25:
                    break
            dz = u * (G - float(np.dot(u, G)))
            z = adam.step(z, dz, lr)
            z = np.clip(z - z.mean(), -80.0, 80.0)
            u = _softmax(z)
            u /= u.sum()
        s = _score_fft(u)
        if s < best_s:
            best_s, best_u = s, u
    return best_u, best_s


def _lp_move(u, K, hard_deadline):
    n = u.size
    c = _fft_conv(u)
    M = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None, np.inf

    top = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
    thr = np.nonzero(c >= 0.95 * M)[0]
    idx = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, thr)))
    if idx.size > K:
        vals = c[idx]
        keep = np.argsort(vals)[-K:]
        idx = idx[keep]
    K = int(idx.size)
    if K <= 0:
        return None, np.inf

    A = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for i, k in enumerate(idx):
        k = int(k)
        a = max(0, k - n + 1)
        b = min(k, n - 1)
        if b >= a:
            A[i, a:b + 1] = u[a:b + 1][::-1]

    bounds = np.zeros((n, 2), dtype=np.float64)
    bounds[:, 1] = 100.0
    remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining < 5.0:
        return None, np.inf
    try:
        res = linprog(
            -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=np.full(K, M, dtype=np.float64),
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": max(1.0, min(25.0, remaining - 10.0))},
        )
    except Exception:
        return None, np.inf

    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
        return None, np.inf
    v = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if v is None or v.size != n:
        return None, np.inf
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, None)
    vs = float(v.sum())
    if vs < 1.0 + 1e-7 or not np.isfinite(vs):
        return None, np.inf
    v /= vs

    best = None
    best_s = np.inf
    for step in (0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20):
        cand = (1.0 - step) * u + step * v
        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, None)
        cs = float(cand.sum())
        if cs < 1e-8:
            continue
        cand /= cs
        s = _score_fft(cand)
        if s < best_s:
            best_s = s
            best = cand
    return best, best_s


def _lp_polish(u, hard_deadline):
    if u is None:
        return None, np.inf
    best_u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    s = float(best_u.sum())
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        best_u /= s
    best_s = _score_fft(best_u)

    K = 384
    no_improve = 0
    while time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 8.0 and no_improve < 10:
        v, s = _lp_move(best_u, K, hard_deadline)
        if v is not None and s < best_s - 1e-10:
            best_u = v
            best_s = s
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
        K = max(128, int(0.8 * K))
    return best_u, best_s


def _random_polish(u, deadline, seed):
    if u is None:
        return None, np.inf
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 17)
    n = u.size
    best_u = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    s = float(best_u.sum())
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        best_u /= s
    best_s = _score_fft(best_u)
    stall = 0

    wlo = max(4, n // 250)
    whi = max(8, n // 12)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cand = best_u.copy()
        w = int(rng.integers(wlo, whi + 1))
        st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
        no = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.16))
        cand[st:st + w] *= float(np.exp(no))
        if rng.random() < 0.22:
            cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])
        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, None)
        cs = float(cand.sum())
        if cs < 1e-8:
            continue
        cand /= cs
        s = _score_fft(cand)
        if s < best_s - 1e-12:
            best_s = s
            best_u = cand
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 350:
                break
    return best_u, best_s


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    hard_deadline = t0 + budget - 2.0
    span = max(25.0, budget - 25.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    n = 5632
    M = 256
    L = n // M

    parent = None
    try:
        p = _clean(height_sequence_1)
        if p is not None:
            parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    d_sine = t0 + min(95.0, 0.10 * span)
    d_block = t0 + max(d_sine + 10.0, min(420.0, 0.30 * span))
    d_full = t0 + max(d_block + 10.0, min(1050.0, 0.70 * span))
    d_lp = t0 + max(d_full + 10.0, min(1300.0, 0.84 * span))
    d_rand = t0 + min(span + 5.0, budget - 10.0)
    d_sine = min(d_sine, hard_deadline - 5.0)
    d_block = min(d_block, hard_deadline - 5.0)
    d_full = min(d_full, hard_deadline - 5.0)
    d_lp = min(d_lp, hard_deadline - 5.0)
    d_rand = min(d_rand, hard_deadline - 2.0)

    best_n = None
    best_s = np.inf

    if parent is not None:
        sp = _score_fft(parent)
        if np.isfinite(sp) and parent.size == n:
            best_n = parent
            best_s = sp

    u_sine, s_sine = _optimize_sine(n, d_sine, rng)
    if u_sine is not None and np.isfinite(s_sine) and s_sine < best_s:
        best_n, best_s = u_sine, s_sine

    u_block, s_block = _optimize_block(best_n, M, L, d_block)
    if u_block is not None and np.isfinite(s_block) and s_block < best_s:
        best_n, best_s = u_block, s_block

    u_full, s_full = _optimize_full(best_n, d_full)
    if u_full is not None and np.isfinite(s_full) and s_full < best_s:
        best_n, best_s = u_full, s_full

    if d_lp > time.monotonic():
        u_lp, s_lp = _lp_polish(best_n, d_lp)
        if u_lp is not None and np.isfinite(s_lp) and s_lp < best_s:
            best_n, best_s = u_lp, s_lp

    if d_rand > time.monotonic():
        u_rand, s_rand = _random_polish(best_n, d_rand, seed)
        if u_rand is not None and np.isfinite(s_rand) and s_rand < best_s:
            best_n, best_s = u_rand, s_rand

    candidates = [best_n]
    if parent is not None:
        candidates.append(parent)
    if best_n is None:
        candidates.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    final_best = None
    final_score = np.inf
    for cand in candidates:
        cu = _clean(cand)
        if cu is None:
            continue
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(cu.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            sc = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_score:
            final_score = sc
            final_best = cu

    if final_best is None:
        final_best = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    return [float(x) for x in final_best]
```
